# S-31 individual decision acceptance matrix

This is the Cycle 1 expansion of the authoritative 714-row requirements ledger.
It does not reinterpret a decision: the decision title and final disposition below
are copied from `requirements-challenge.md`; each row links the earlier owner
record and names the exact final evidence record(s) that can accept it.
`PROV`, `INV`, and `REL` remain open until their separately authorized evidence
exists on the frozen SHA. `BILL` accepts only disabled mode plus the S-18 handoff.

## Exact evidence records

| Record | Exact command or artifact |
|---|---|
| REQ | `python3 bootstrap-scaffold/validate-requirements-ledger.py`; the individual IR `### Decision`; this row; linked owner TDD record(s). |
| REP | S-31 section 13 classified residue searches; `git diff --check`; `make preflight`; `scripts/pr-preflight --pr-body-file <body>`. |
| BE | `backend/test.sh`; `PROVIDER_MODE=offline make dev-up`; `make dev-check USER=alice`; `backend/testing/e2e/run.sh`; route/OpenAPI/runtime/workflow contract manifests. |
| MAC | `desktop/macos/test.sh`; `desktop/macos/scripts/agent-logic-harness.sh`; `OMI_APP_NAME=omi-wave6-s31 ./run.sh`; Desktop Core T0/T2/fault manifests with matching `sourceGitSHA`. |
| PROV | BL-001 natural-audio PTT record; `agent-continuity-gauntlet.sh --suite all`; `check-gauntlet-evidence-at-head.sh`; approved deployed mint/direct-provider probe. **OPEN.** |
| INV | BL-002 verified read-only inventory; separately authorized exact-SHA development deploy, health/auth/listen/account-deletion/rollback records. **OPEN.** |
| BILL | Entry/final `BILLING_MODE=disabled` backend/Mac/network-recorder evidence and `bootstrap-scaffold/dodo-integration.md`; no Dodo activation. |
| REL | S-29 signed/notarized artifact, intake, qualification, preview, promotion/recovery/rollback manifests bound to the frozen SHA/digest. **OPEN.** |
